## collab/apply.ts
type DocApplyOptions = ApplyOptions & {
    key?: KeyGen;
    label?: string;
    noLog?: boolean;
    parentBounds?: Bounds;
    prefabWriter?: PrefabDocWriter;
};
type DocApplyResult = (Extract<ApplyResult, {
    ok: true;
}> & {
    docs: PrefabDocWrite[];
    keys: string[];
    refKeys: Record<Ref, string>;
}) | Extract<ApplyResult, {
    ok: false;
}>;
type PrefabChange = {
    changed: readonly string[];
    next: PrefabLibrary;
    prev: PrefabLibrary;
};
type PrefabDocPlan = {
    issue: SceneIssue;
    ok: false;
} | {
    ok: true;
    write(origin: DocOrigin, ctx: {
        label: string;
        scene: LogEntry | null;
    }): PrefabDocWrite[];
};
type PrefabDocWrite = {
    doc: string;
    entry: string;
};
type PrefabDocWriter = {
    plan(change: PrefabChange): PrefabDocPlan;
};
declare const SCENE_ONLY_OPS: readonly SceneOp["op"][];
declare function applyOpsToDoc(doc: Y.Doc, batch: OpBatch, origin: SceneOrigin, registry: Registry, opts?: DocApplyOptions): DocApplyResult;
declare function headerOf(def: SceneDefinition): Record<string, unknown>;
declare function patchGroup(ymap: Y.Map<unknown>, prev: GroupDef | undefined, next: GroupDef): void;
declare function refsOfView(view: DocView): Map<string, Ref>;

type CloseReason = "reload" | "retry" | "revoked" | "too-large";

    };

};
type ProjectConnection = {
    blobs?: ProjectBlobs;

};
type ProjectConnectionState = {
    access: ProjectAccess;

};

    closed(code: null | number, socketOpen: boolean, reason?: string): void;

};

    destroy(): void;

    subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void;

        at: string;
        text: string;
    };
    version: number;
};
type SceneHome = {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    project: ProjectId;
};
type UploadedBlob = {
    bytes: number;
    sha256: Sha256;
};
type UsageLevel = "full" | "ok" | "warn";
type UsageLineView = {
    detail?: string;
    level: UsageLevel;
    max: number;
    quota: string;
    ratio: number;
    text: string;
    used: number;
};

    };

## editor-ui/pending/pending.ts
type Pending = {
    busy(scope: string): PendingTask | undefined;
    hold(spec: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
        scope: string;
    }): (outcome?: {
        error?: unknown;
        errorTitle?: string;
    }) => void;
    readonly store: Store<{
        tasks: readonly PendingTask[];
    }>;
    run<T>(spec: PendingRun<T>, work: (step: PendingStep) => Promise<T>): Promise<{
        error: unknown;
        ok: false;
    } | {
        ok: true;
        value: T;
    }>;
};
type PendingRun<T> = {
    error: ((error: unknown) => string) | string;
    id?: string;
    label: string;
    scope: string;
    success?: ((value: T) => null | string) | string;
    total?: number;
};
type PendingStep = {
    label(text: string): void;
    progress(done: number, total?: number): void;
};
type PendingTask = {
    id: string;
    label: string;
    progress: null | {
        done: number;
        total: number;
    };
    scope: string;
    startedAt: number;
};
declare const createPending: (opts: {
    notify: EditorNotify;
    now?: () => number;
}) => Pending;

    value: unknown;
    widget: FieldWidget;
};
type InspectorSectionId = (typeof INSPECTOR_SECTIONS)[number];
type InspectorSectionModel = {
    behavior?: BehaviorSectionInfo;
    fields: ModelField[];
    id: ModelSectionId;
    title: string;
};

    instance: string;
    nested?: string;

    target: RefTarget;
};

    editable: boolean;
    flip: boolean;
    ref: Ref;
    rot: 0 | 1 | 2 | 3;
    z: number;
};
type ModelEdit = {
    error: string;
    ok: false;
} | {
    ok: true;
    ops: SceneOp[];
};
type ModelField = Omit<InspectorRow, "children"> & {
    children?: ModelField[];
    group?: string;
    section: ModelSectionId;
};
type ModelSectionId = BehaviorSectionId | InspectorSectionId;
type ParamModel = {
    description: string;
    field: ModelField;
    name: string;
    set: boolean;
    targets: readonly OverridePath[];
};
declare const SECTION_TITLES: Readonly<Record<InspectorSectionId, string>>;
type ShapeConversion = {
    lossless: boolean;
    note?: string;
    to: ShapeType;
    tuple: ShapeTuple;
};
declare function buildInspectorModel(registry: Registry, targets: readonly RefTarget[], opts?: {
    def?: SceneDefinition;
}): InspectorModel;
declare function instanceParamsModel(def: SceneDefinition, ref: Ref, registry: Registry, prefabs: PrefabLibrary): InstanceParamsModel | null;
declare function instanceTransformModel(def: SceneDefinition, ref: Ref, prefabs: PrefabLibrary): InstanceTransformModel | null;

    ok: true;
} | {
    error: string;
    ok: false;
};

    ok: false;
};

} | {
    error: string;
    ok: false;
};

## editor/keymap.ts
type Direction = "down" | "left" | "right" | "up";
declare const ENGINE_KEYS: readonly EngineKeyBinding[];
type EditorCommand = "bring-forward" | "cancel-gesture" | "clear-selection" | "clear-tool" | "copy" | "cut" | "debug" | "delete" | "delete-points" | "duplicate" | "faster" | "flip-x" | "flip-y" | "frame" | "group" | "hierarchy-down" | "hierarchy-up" | "leave-prefab" | "leave-sub-object" | "pan-hold" | "paste" | "pause" | "play" | "play-here" | "redo" | "rotate-ccw" | "rotate-cw" | "save" | "select-all" | "send-backward" | "slower" | "step" | "stop" | "toggle-snap" | "tool:box" | "tool:circle" | "tool:pan" | "tool:pen" | "tool:rotate" | "tool:select" | "tool:waypoints" | "turn-ccw" | "turn-cw" | "undo" | "ungroup" | `nudge-${Direction}-coarse` | `nudge-${Direction}-fine` | `nudge-${Direction}`;

    altKey: boolean;
    ctrlKey: boolean;
    key: string;
    metaKey: boolean;
    shiftKey: boolean;
};

    command: EditorCommand;
    description: string;
    key: string;
    scope: KeyScope;
    when?: (ctx: KeyContext) => boolean;
};

    editableTarget: boolean;
    gesture?: boolean;
    hasSelection?: boolean;
    inPrefab?: boolean;
    mode: EditorMode;
    quarterTurns?: boolean;
    subObject?: boolean;
    tool?: string;
};
type KeyMatch = {
    binding: EngineKeyBinding;
    command: EditorCommand;
    source: "engine";
} | {
    binding: GameKeyBinding;
    command: string;
    source: "game";
};
type KeyScope = "edit" | "global" | "play" | "sub-object" | "text-field";
type Keymap = {
    bindings: readonly (EngineKeyBinding | GameKeyBinding)[];
    chordOf(command: EditorCommand): string | undefined;
    resolve(e: EditorKeyEvent, ctx: KeyContext): KeyMatch | null;
    shadowed: readonly GameKeyBinding[];
};

declare const DEFAULT_SNAP: SnapSettings;
type EditorTool = {
    cancel?(): boolean;

    gesture?(): boolean;
    key?(e: ToolKeyEvent): boolean;

};
type EditorToolContext = {
    commit(batch: OpBatch, opts?: {
        selectionAfter?: Ref[];
    }): boolean;

    hover?(ref: Ref | null): void;
    index(): SpatialIndex;

        hidden: ReadonlySet<string>;
        locked: ReadonlySet<string>;

    scale(): number;

        by: Pt;

    }): void;

};

    at: Pt;
    cursor?: string;
    id: string;
    kind: HandleKind;
};

    aabb: Aabb;

    key: string;
    layer: string;
    ref: Ref;
    sprite?: ShapeObj;
    type: string;
    z: number;
};
type OverlayTone = "ghost" | "guide" | "hover" | "invalid" | "selected";

    0.25,
    0.5,
    1,
    2,
    4
];

    angleDeg: number;

    geometry: boolean;

};

};

    key: string;
    repeat: boolean;
    type: "down" | "up";
};

    alt: boolean;
    mod: boolean;
    shift: boolean;
};

        description?: string;
        label: string;
        value: string;
    }[];

    label: string;
    value: string;
};

        to: Pt;

        hits?: Ref[];
        rect: Aabb;

        batch: OpBatch;

        preview?: boolean;

    entry: string;
    files: readonly {
        bytes: number;
        path: string;
        role: "code" | "style" | "wasm";
        sha256: Sha256;
    }[];
    rapier: ReleaseManifest["build"]["rapier"];
    thirdParty: readonly ThirdPartyLicence[];
};
type ContentPlan = {
    assets: Readonly<Record<AssetId, ReleaseAsset>>;
    credits: Omit<ReleaseManifest["credits"], "code">;
    issues: readonly SceneIssue[];
    needsRapier: boolean;
    projectBundle: ReleaseManifest["projectBundle"];
    pwa: ReleaseManifest["pwa"];
    scenes: readonly ReleaseScene[];
    start: AssetId;
    time: ReleaseManifest["time"];
};
type ExportInput = {
    engine: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
    game: GameDefinition;
    kinds: KindRegistry;
    project: ProjectFile;
    registry: Registry;
    snapshot: ProjectSnapshot;
    target: ReleaseTarget;
    texts: ReadonlyMap<AssetId, string>;
    view: ProjectView;
};
type ExportPlan = {
    files: readonly PlannedFile[];
    issues: readonly SceneIssue[];
    manifest: ReleaseManifest;
};
type PlannedFile = {
    path: string;
    role: ReleaseFile["role"];
    source: {
        bytes: Uint8Array;
        kind: "bytes";
    } | {
        kind: "blob";
        sha256: Sha256;
    } | {
        kind: "built";
        path: string;
    } | {
        kind: "text";
        text: string;
    };
};
declare function finishExport(content: ContentPlan, code: BuiltCode, input: ExportInput, budget?: Partial<ExportBudget>): Promise<ExportPlan>;

## game/behaviors.ts
declare const BEHAVIORS_FIELD = "behaviors";
declare const BEHAVIOR_NAME_PATTERN: RegExp;
type BehaviorBlock = Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
type BehaviorCategory = NonNullable<NonNullable<BehaviorDef["inspector"]>["category"]>;
type BehaviorDef<F extends z.ZodRawShape = z.ZodRawShape> = {
    conflicts?: readonly BehaviorName[];
    description: string;
    fields: ((env: FieldEnv) => F) | F;
    inspector?: {
        category?: "movement" | "physics" | "visual" | "world";
        label?: string;
        order?: readonly string[];
    };
    name: BehaviorName;
    refine?(block: BehaviorBlock): string | undefined;
    requires?: readonly BehaviorName[];
    system?: BehaviorSystemFactory;
    template?: BehaviorBlock;
    visualOnly?: boolean;
};
type BehaviorName = string;
interface BehaviorSystem<B extends BehaviorBlock = BehaviorBlock> {
    attach(entityId: string, block: B, entity: RuntimeEntity): void;
    detach(entityId: string): void;
    update?(entityId: string, block: B, prev: B, entity: RuntimeEntity): void;
    beforeStep?(dtMs: number): void;
    beforePhysics?(): void;
    step?(dtMs: number): void;
    render?(alpha: number): void;
    reset?(): void;
    seek?(ms: number): void;
    clear(): void;
    size(): number;
    ready?(ids: readonly string[]): void;
    disable?(entityId: string): void;
    enable?(entityId: string): void;
    save?(entityId: string): JsonValue | undefined;
    restore?(entityId: string, state: JsonValue): void;
}
type BehaviorSystemFactory = (ctx: EngineContext, def: RegisteredBehavior) => BehaviorSystem;
type RegisteredBehavior = BehaviorDef & {
    index: number;
    origin: string;
    shape: z.ZodRawShape;
};
declare function defineBehavior<F extends z.ZodRawShape>(def: BehaviorDef<F>): BehaviorDef<F>;

## game/define.ts
type CreateRegistryOptions = {
    assets?: FieldEnv["assets"];
    materials?: Readonly<Record<string, ProjectMaterialDef>>;
};
type EntityOf<D extends EntityTypeDef> = D extends EntityTypeDef<infer F> ? RuntimeEntity<z.infer<z.ZodObject<F>>> : RuntimeEntity;
type ProjectMaterialDef = {
    blend?: BlendMode;
    description: string;
    physics?: {
        friction: number;
        impulse?: Vec2;

        targets: readonly EntityTypeName[];
    }[];
    spawnOnly?: true;
    style?: Style;
    system?: SystemFactory;
    type: EntityTypeName;
};
interface EventBus {

    once(type: string, handler: (e: StepEvent) => void, opts?: EventSubscribeOptions): () => void;
}
type EventSubscribeOptions = {
    entity?: string;
    owner?: string;
};

    actions: readonly ActionDef[];

    checks?: Readonly<Record<string, CheckKindDef>>;

        collidesWith: readonly string[];
    }>>;
    commands?: Readonly<Record<string, CommandDef>>;
    configHash?: string;
    createRuntime(ctx: EngineContext): GameRuntime<S>;
    editor?: GameEditorDef;
    editorKeys?: readonly GameKeyBinding[];
    id: string;
    input?: GameInputDef;
    issues?: Readonly<Record<string, IssueCodeDef>>;
    layers?: readonly SceneLayerDef[];
    materials?: Readonly<Record<string, MaterialDef>>;
    persist?: GamePersistDef;

    projectSettings?: z.ZodRawShape;
    resolution?: Partial<ResolutionPolicy>;
    saves?: GameSavesDef;
    settings?: z.ZodRawShape;
    spawnOrder?: readonly (EntityTypeName | readonly EntityTypeName[])[];
    tables?: Readonly<Record<string, GameTableDef>>;
    template?: SceneTemplate;
    time?: TimeModel;
    types: readonly EntityTypeDef[];
    validators?: readonly SceneValidator[];
    world?: GameWorldDef;
};

interface SceneSystem<E extends RuntimeEntity = RuntimeEntity> {
    spawn(e: E): void;
    despawn(id: string): void;

    beforePhysics?(): void;
    afterStep?(dtMs: number): void;
    ready?(ids: readonly string[]): void;
    disable?(id: string): void;
    enable?(id: string): void;
    save?(id: string): JsonValue | undefined;
    restore?(id: string, state: JsonValue): void;
}

    hw: number;

    r: number;
    type: "circle";
}) & {
    cast?: "gravity" | "none" | Pt;

    alpha?: number;

    strokeWidth?: number;
};

        owner?: string;

        owner?: string;
    }): number;

}

type Cell = readonly [
    col: number,
    row: number
];

    c0: number;
    c1: number;
    r0: number;
    r1: number;
};
type CellKey = number;
interface GridGeometry {
    readonly settings: GridSettings;
    neighbors(c: Cell, diagonals?: boolean): Cell[];
    stepCost(a: Cell, b: Cell): number;
    heuristic(a: Cell, b: Cell): number;
    distance(a: Cell, b: Cell, diagonals?: boolean): number;
    center(c: Cell): Pt;
    cellAt(p: Pt): Cell;
    corners(c: Cell): Pt[];
    line(a: Cell, b: Cell): Cell[];
    cellsIn(shape: ShapeObj): Cell[];
    rect(bounds: Bounds): CellBounds;
}
declare function boundsCells(b: CellBounds): Cell[];
declare function cellKey(c: Cell): CellKey;
declare function cellOf(k: CellKey): Cell;
declare function gridGeometry(settings: GridSettings): GridGeometry;

declare const sameCell: (a: Cell, b: Cell) => boolean;

## headless/playtest-types.ts
type CompileResult = {
    issues: ScriptIssue[];
    ok: false;
} | {
    ok: true;
    script: CompiledInputScript;
};
type CompiledInputScript = {
    edges: InputEdge[] | null;
    maxTicks: number;
    play: PlayOptions;
    scene?: string;
    steps: CompiledStep[];
};
type CompiledStep = {
    commands?: GameCommand[];
    hold: string[];
    index: number;
    max?: number;
    ticks?: number;
    until?: UntilClause;
};
type EventMatch = string | {
    entityId?: string;
    other?: string;
    type: string;
};
type FuzzLegal = {
    empty: number;
    sent: number;
};
type FuzzOptions<S = unknown, G = unknown> = {
    dev?: boolean;
    fromSave?: SaveStart;
    game?: GameDefinition<S, G> | null;
    library?: PrefabLibrary;
    play?: PlayOptions<G>;
    prefabs?: PrefabLibrary;
    project?: ProjectRuntimeView | null;
    rapier?: RapierModule | null;
    registry?: Registry;
    rollouts?: number;
    scene: SceneDefinition;
    seed: number | string;
    ticks?: number;
};
type FuzzReport = {
    blocked: false;
    configHash: string;
    legal?: FuzzLegal;
    results: Record<RunResult, number>;
    rollouts: number;
    scene: string;
    sceneHash: SceneHash;
    seed: number | string;
    ticks: number;
    ticksPerRollout: number;
    timing: {
        elapsedMs: number;
        ticksPerSecond: number;
    };
    touched: FuzzTouch[];
    untouched: Ref[];
};
type FuzzRun = FuzzReport | PlaytestBlocked;
type FuzzTouch = {
    firstRollout: number;
    firstTick: number;
    reached: number;
    ref: Ref;
    type: EntityTypeName;
};
type InputEdge = [
    tick: number,
    action: string,
    pressed: 0 | 1
];
type InputScript<G = unknown> = {
    $schema?: string;
    at?: Pt;
    game?: G;
    maxTicks?: number;
    scene?: string;
    seed?: number | string;
    steps: InputScriptStep[];
    timeOffsetMs?: number;
    v: 1;
};
type InputScriptStep = ({
    hold: string[];
} & StepDuration) | {
    args?: unknown;
    command: string;
} | {
    elapse: number;
} | {
    press: string | string[];
} | {
    wait: number;
};
type InputScriptStepV2 = ({
    hold: string[];
    set?: AnalogSet;
} & StepDuration) | ({
    pointer: ScriptPointer;
} & StepDuration) | ({
    set: AnalogSet;
} & StepDuration) | {
    args?: unknown;
    command: string;
} | {
    elapse: number;
} | {
    join: DeviceId;
} | {
    leave: DeviceId;
} | {
    press: string | string[];
} | {
    wait: number;
};
type InputScriptTrack = {
    player: number;
    steps: InputScriptStepV2[];
};
type InputScriptV2<G = unknown> = Omit<InputScript<G>, "steps" | "v"> & {
    players?: number;
    v: 2;
} & ({
    steps: InputScriptStepV2[];
    tracks?: never;
} | {
    steps?: never;
    tracks: InputScriptTrack[];
});
type PersistMatch = {
    eq?: JsonValue;
    gte?: number;
    lte?: number;
    pointer: string;
};
type PlayOptions<G = unknown> = {
    at?: Vec2;
    game?: G;
    seed?: number | string;
    timeOffsetMs?: number;
};
type PlaytestBlocked = {
    blocked: true;
    issues: SceneIssue[];
    load?: LoadReport;
    scene: string;
    scriptIssues: ScriptIssue[];
    stage: "load" | "run" | "scene" | "script";
};
type PlaytestOptions<S = unknown, G = unknown> = {
    dev?: boolean;
    flow?: SceneLibrary;
    game?: GameDefinition<S, G> | null;
    library?: PrefabLibrary;
    maxEvents?: number;
    maxTrail?: number;
    prefabs?: PrefabLibrary;
    project?: ProjectRuntimeView | null;
    rapier?: RapierModule | null;
    registry?: Registry;
    scene: SceneDefinition;
    script: CompiledInputScript | InputScript<G> | InputScriptV2<G>;
    timing?: RunTiming;
};
type PlaytestOutcomeReport = {
    at: Vec2 | null;
    cause?: string;
    entityId?: string;
    result: RunResult;
    tick: number;
    to?: AssetId;
};
type PlaytestReport<S = unknown> = {
    blocked: false;
    commands: RecordingCommand[];
    configHash: string;
    events: StepEvent[];
    final: S | null;
    inputs: InputEdge[];
    issues: SceneIssue[];
    legal?: GameCommand[];
    legalTruncated?: boolean;
    outcome: PlaytestOutcomeReport;
    recording: RunRecording;
    scene: string;
    sceneHash: SceneHash;
    segments?: PlaytestSegment[];
    slots: number;
    steps: PlaytestStep[];
    stepsReached: number;
    stream: RecordingStream;
    tickRate: number;
    ticks: number;
    trail: {
        every: 4;
        pts: Pt[];
        truncated: boolean;
    };
    truncated: boolean;
};
type PlaytestRun<S = unknown> = PlaytestBlocked | PlaytestReport<S>;
type PlaytestSegment = {
    end: PlaytestSegmentEnd;
    recording: RunRecording;
    scene: AssetId;
    ticks: number;
};
type PlaytestSegmentEnd = {
    entry?: string;
    result: RunResult;
    tick: number;
    to?: AssetId;
};
type PlaytestStep = {
    end: "done" | "max" | "run" | "until";
    index: number;
    player?: number;
    start: number;
    ticks: number;
};
type ScriptIssue = {
    expected?: string;
    got?: string;
    hint?: string;
    message: string;
    path: string;
};
type StepDuration = {
    for?: number;

};

    exists?: ExistsMatch;
    near?: NearMatch;
    persist?: PersistMatch;
    scene?: AssetId;
    tick?: number;
};

type ReplayOptions<S = unknown> = RecordingHost & {
    dev?: boolean;
    flow?: SceneLibrary;
    game?: GameDefinition<S> | null;
    library?: PrefabLibrary;
    prefabs?: PrefabLibrary;
    project?: ProjectRuntimeView | null;

    sceneId?: AssetId;
};
type ReplayReport<S = unknown> = {
    blocked: false;
    divergence: null | string;
    final: S | null;
    issues: SceneIssue[];
    matches: boolean;
    migratedFrom?: 1;
    recording: RunRecording;
    stale: boolean;
    warnings: string[];
};
type ReplayRun<S = unknown> = PlaytestBlocked | ReplayReport<S>;

    description: string;
    empty: () => ChecksFile;
    format: (file: ChecksFile) => string;
    kind: string;
    layout: {
        readonly as: "map";

            readonly checks: {
                readonly as: "keyed";
                readonly item: {
                    readonly as: "map";
                    readonly fields: {
                        readonly kind: {
                            as: "atomic";
                        };
                    };
                    readonly rest: {
                        as: "atomic";
                    };
                };
            };
        };
        readonly rest: {
            as: "atomic";
        };
    };
    match: {
        suffixes: string[];
    };
    migrations: Readonly<Record<number, Migration>>;

    schema: (env: KindEnv) => z.ZodType<ChecksFile>;

## project/kinds/define.ts
type AssetKindDef = BlobKindDef<any, any> | CodeKindDef | DocKindDef<any>;
type AssetMatch = {
    jsonKind?: string;
    suffixes?: readonly string[];
    test?: (json: unknown) => boolean;
};
type BlobKindDef<M = unknown, S = unknown> = KindBase & {
    contentTypes: readonly string[];
    inspect(bytes: Uint8Array, settings: S, env: InspectEnv): InspectResult<M>;
    maxBytes: number;
    meta: z.ZodType<M>;
    refs?(entry: AssetEntry): readonly RefSite[];
    settings: z.ZodType<S>;
    storage: "blob";
};
type CodeKindDef = KindBase & {
    storage: "code";
};
type DocKindDef<J = unknown> = KindBase & {
    empty(env: KindEnv, init: {
        id: AssetId;
    }): J;
    format?(value: J): string;
    idField?: string;
    layout: "custom" | DocLayout;
    limits?: {
        maxItems?: number;
        maxStateBytes: number;
    };
    migrations?: Readonly<Record<number, Migration>>;
    parse?(input: unknown, env: KindEnv): ParseAssetResult<J>;
    perProject?: 1;
    perProjectWords?: PerProjectWords;
    prefix?: "asset" | "scene";
    refs?(value: J, env: KindEnv): readonly RefSite[];
    schema(env: KindEnv): z.ZodType<J>;
    schemaId: string;
    storage: "doc";
    validate?(value: J, env: KindEnv): readonly SceneIssue[];
    version: number;
};
type DocLayout = {
    as: "atomic";
} | {
    as: "keyed";
    id?: string;
    item: Extract<DocLayout, {
        as: "map";
    }>;
} | {
    as: "list";
    item: DocLayout;
} | {
    as: "map";
    fields?: Readonly<Record<string, DocLayout>>;
    rest?: DocLayout;
} | {
    as: "text";
};
type InspectEnv = {
    idAt(path: AssetPath): AssetId | undefined;
    path: AssetPath;
};
type InspectResult<M> = {
    import?: Record<string, unknown>;
    meta: M;
    ok: true;
} | {
    issue: SceneIssue;
    ok: false;
};
type KindBase = {
    defaultFolder?: (env: KindEnv, near?: AssetPath) => string;
    description: string;
    kind: AssetKindName;
    match: AssetMatch;
    title: string;
};
type KindEnv = {
    inputMap?: Pick<InputMapFile, "players"> | null;
    prefab?: MigrateEnv["prefab"];
    project: ProjectView | null;
    registry: Registry;
};
type ParseAssetResult<J = unknown> = {
    issues: SceneIssue[];
    migratedFrom?: number;
    ok: true;
    value: J;
} | {
    issues: SceneIssue[];
    ok: false;
};
type PerProjectWords = {
    contents?: string;
    held?: string;
    noun: string;
};
declare const byCodeUnit: (a: string, b: string) => number;
declare function defineAssetKind<K extends AssetKindDef>(def: K): K;

    at: (number | string)[];

        y: number
    ];

                    as: "map";
                    fields: {
                        bindings: {
                            as: "list";
                            item: {
                                as: "atomic";
                            };
                        };
                    };
                    rest: {
                        as: "atomic";
                    };
                };
            };
            players: {
                as: "map";
                rest: {
                    as: "atomic";
                };
            };
            touch: {
                as: "keyed";
                item: {
                    as: "map";
                    rest: {
                        as: "atomic";
                    };
                };
            };
        };
        rest: {
            as: "atomic";
        };

    limits: {
        maxStateBytes: number;

## project/ops.ts
type BlobInspect = (kind: BlobKindDef, entry: AssetEntry) => InspectResult<unknown>;
type EditSceneOp = Extract<ProjectOp, {
    op: "editScene";
}>;

    before?: number | string;

    value: unknown;

} | {
    set: JsonPointer;
    value: unknown;

declare const PREFAB_SCENE_OPS: readonly SceneOp["op"][];
declare const PROJECT_OPS_SCHEMA_ID = "urn:jams-engine:project-ops:v1";
declare const PROJECT_OP_NAMES: readonly [
    "createAsset",
    "importAsset",
    "renameAsset",
    "moveAsset",
    "deleteAsset",
    "restoreAsset",
    "purgeAsset",
    "changeAssetId",
    "setImport",

    "editScene"

type ProjectApplyResult = {
    created: AssetId[];
    docs: string[];
    entry?: ProjectLogEntry;
    issues: SceneIssue[];
    ok: true;
} | {
    issue: SceneIssue;
    ok: false;
    opIndex: number;
};
type ProjectBatchCheck = {
    created: AssetId[];
    delegated: number[];
    issues: SceneIssue[];
    ok: true;
    purged: AssetKey[];
    registry: boolean;
    scenes: ReadonlyMap<AssetKey, SceneWrite>;
    state: ProjectState;
    values: ReadonlyMap<AssetKey, unknown>;
    written: AssetKey[];
    writtenBy: ReadonlyMap<AssetKey, number>;
} | {
    issue: SceneIssue;
    ok: false;
    opIndex: number;
};
type ProjectBatchEnv = {
    by: string;
    inspect?: BlobInspect;
    kinds: KindRegistry;
    newKey: () => AssetKey;
    now: number;
    prefabEdit?: (op: EditSceneOp, ctx: {
        entry: AssetEntry;
        index: number;
    }) => SceneIssue | null;
    prefabsFor?: (scene: AssetId) => PrefabLibrary;
    registry: Registry;
};
type ProjectOp = {
    bytes: number;
    id: AssetId;
    op: "replaceContent";
    sha256: Sha256;
} | {
    bytes: number;
    id?: AssetId;
    kind?: AssetKindName;
    licence?: Licence;
    op: "importAsset";
    path: AssetPath;
    settings?: Record<string, unknown>;
    sha256: Sha256;
} | {
    content?: unknown;
    id?: AssetId;
    kind: AssetKindName;
    licence?: Licence;
    op: "createAsset";
    path: AssetPath;
} | {
    edits: JsonEdit[];
    id: AssetId;
    op: "editAsset";
} | {
    file?: string;
    id: AssetId;
    name?: null | string;
    op: "renameAsset";
} | {
    force?: boolean;
    id: AssetId;
    op: "deleteAsset";
} | {
    id: AssetId;
    licence: Licence | null;
    op: "setLicence";
} | {
    id: AssetId;
    op: "changeAssetId";
    to: AssetId;
} | {
    id: AssetId;
    op: "editScene";
    ops: SceneOp[];
} | {
    id: AssetId;
    op: "purgeAsset";
} | {
    id: AssetId;
    op: "restoreAsset";
} | {
    id: AssetId;
    op: "setImport";
    set: Record<string, unknown>;
} | {
    ids: AssetId[];
    op: "moveAsset";
    to: string;
};
type ProjectOpBatch = {
    base?: Sha256;
    label?: string;
    ops: ProjectOp[];
    v: 1;
};
type ProjectOpName = ProjectOp["op"];
declare const SCENE_KIND_NAME = "scene";
type SceneWrite = {
    ops: SceneOp[];
    prefabs?: PrefabLibrary;
    registry: Registry;
};
declare function checkProjectBatch(state: ProjectState, docs: ReadonlyMap<AssetId, unknown>, batch: ProjectOpBatch, env: ProjectBatchEnv): ProjectBatchCheck;

    ops: z.ZodArray<z.ZodDiscriminatedUnion<readonly [
        z.ZodObject<{

            licence: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodObject<{

                author: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
                source: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;

            }, z.core.$strict>>;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"createAsset">;
            path: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            bytes: z.ZodNumber;
            id: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
            kind: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
            licence: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodObject<{
                attribution: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
                author: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
                source: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
                spdx: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
            }, z.core.$strict>>;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"importAsset">;
            path: z.ZodString;
            settings: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodUnknown>>;
            sha256: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            file: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
            id: z.ZodString;
            name: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodUnion<readonly [
                z.ZodString,
                z.ZodNull
            ]>>;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"renameAsset">;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            ids: z.ZodArray<z.ZodString>;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"moveAsset">;
            to: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            force: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodBoolean>;
            id: z.ZodString;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"deleteAsset">;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            id: z.ZodString;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"restoreAsset">;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            id: z.ZodString;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"purgeAsset">;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            id: z.ZodString;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"changeAssetId">;
            to: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            id: z.ZodString;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"setImport">;
            set: z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodUnknown>;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            bytes: z.ZodNumber;
            id: z.ZodString;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"replaceContent">;
            sha256: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            id: z.ZodString;
            licence: z.ZodUnion<readonly [
                z.ZodObject<{
                    attribution: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
                    author: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
                    source: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
                    spdx: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
                }, z.core.$strict>,
                z.ZodNull
            ]>;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"setLicence">;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            edits: z.ZodArray<z.ZodUnion<readonly [
                z.ZodObject<{
                    set: z.ZodString;
                    value: z.ZodUnknown;
                }, z.core.$strict>,
                z.ZodObject<{
                    unset: z.ZodString;
                }, z.core.$strict>,
                z.ZodObject<{
                    before: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodUnion<readonly [
                        z.ZodString,
                        z.ZodNumber
                    ]>>;
                    insert: z.ZodString;
                    value: z.ZodUnknown;
                }, z.core.$strict>,
                z.ZodObject<{
                    remove: z.ZodString;
                }, z.core.$strict>
            ]>>;
            id: z.ZodString;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"editAsset">;
        }, z.core.$strict>,
        z.ZodObject<{
            id: z.ZodString;
            op: z.ZodLiteral<"editScene">;
            ops: z.ZodArray<z.ZodObject<{
                op: z.ZodString;
            }, z.core.$loose>>;
        }, z.core.$strict>
    ], "op">>;
    v: z.ZodLiteral<1>;
}, z.core.$strict>;

declare const CREDITS_FILE = "credits.html";
type CheckReleaseOptions = {
    measureBudget?: (files: readonly ReleaseFile[], content: ReleaseBudgetContent, limits: ExportBudget) => ExportBudgetReport;
};
type ExportBudget = {
    codeBytes: number;
    fileBytes: number;
    firstLoadBytes: number;
    totalBytes: number;
};
type ExportBudgetReport = {
    limits: ExportBudget;
    measured: {
        assetBytes: number;
        codeBytes: number;
        dataBytes: number;
        firstLoadBytes: number;
        largestFile: {
            bytes: number;
            path: string;
        };
        totalBytes: number;
        wasmBytes: number;
    };
    over: readonly (keyof ExportBudget)[];
};
declare const INDEX_HTML_FILE = "index.html";
type ParseReleaseManifestResult = {
    issues: SceneIssue[];
    ok: false;
} | {
    manifest: ReleaseManifest;
    ok: true;
};
declare const RELEASE_FILE = "release.json";
declare const RELEASE_SCHEMA_ID = "urn:jams-engine:release:v1";
declare const RELEASE_VERSION = 1;
type ReleaseAsset = {
    bytes: number;
    file: string;
    import?: Record<string, unknown>;
    key: AssetKey;
    kind: AssetKindName;
    licence?: Licence;
    meta?: Record<string, unknown>;
    name?: string;
    path: AssetPath;
    sha256: Sha256;
    storage: "blob" | "doc";
};
type ReleaseBudgetContent = {
    assets: ReleaseManifest["assets"];
    projectBundle: ReleaseManifest["projectBundle"];
    scenes: readonly ReleaseScene[];
    start: AssetId;
};
type ReleaseCredit = {
    assets: readonly AssetId[];
    attribution?: string;
    author?: string;
    source?: string;
    spdx?: string;
};
type ReleaseFile = {
    bytes: number;
    path: string;
    role: "asset" | "code" | "data" | "icon" | "shell" | "style" | "wasm" | "worker";
    sha256: Sha256;
};
type ReleaseManifest = {
    $schema?: string;
    assets: Readonly<Record<AssetId, ReleaseAsset>>;
    budget: ExportBudgetReport;
    build: {
        entry: string;
        files: readonly ReleaseFile[];
        hash: Sha256;
        rapier: null | {
            bytes: number;
            file: null | string;
            package: "@dimforge/rapier2d" | "@dimforge/rapier2d-compat";
            version: string;
        };
        target: ReleaseTarget;
    };
    credits: {
        assets: readonly ReleaseCredit[];
        code: readonly ThirdPartyLicence[];
        missing: readonly AssetId[];
    };
    engine: {
        agentSurface: number;
        formats: {
            collabLayout: number;
            inputScript: number;
            prefab: number;
            recording: number;
            save: number;
            scene: number;
        };
        version: string;
    };
    flow: {
        scenes: readonly ReleaseScene[];
        start: AssetId;
    };
    game: {
        configHash: null | string;
        id: string;
        saveVersion: null | number;
    };
    kind: "release";
    project: {
        build: Readonly<Record<AssetKindName, Sha256>>;
        id: ProjectId;
        name: string;
        snapshot: Sha256;
        snapshotEngine: string;
    };
    projectBundle: {
        bytes: number;
        entries: readonly AssetId[];
    };
    pwa: null | {
        background: string;
        display: "fullscreen" | "minimal-ui" | "standalone";
        icons: readonly {
            file: string;
            size: 192 | 512;
        }[];
        name: string;
        orientation: "any" | "landscape" | "portrait";
        shortName: string;
        themeColor: string;
    };
    replay: {
        acrossPlatforms: boolean;
    };
    time: {
        catchUp: null | {
            maxMs: number;
        };
        kind: TimeKind;
        rate: number;
        wakeOnPointerMove: boolean;
    };
    v: 1;
};
type ReleaseRecordingRef = Pick<RunRecording, "engine" | "project"> & {
    release?: Sha256;
};
type ReleaseScene = {
    bundle: {
        bytes: number;
        entries: readonly AssetId[];
    };
    id: AssetId;
    physics: "none" | "rapier";
    prefabs: readonly AssetId[];
    sublevel?: true;
    title?: string;
};
type ReleaseTarget = "desktop" | "itch" | "mobile" | "web";
declare const SERVICE_WORKER_FILE = "sw.js";
type ThirdPartyLicence = {
    licence: string;
    notice: null | string;
    package: string;
    version: string;
};
declare const WEB_MANIFEST_FILE = "app.webmanifest";
declare function checkReleaseManifest(manifest: ReleaseManifest, files?: ReadonlyMap<string, Sha256>, options?: CheckReleaseOptions): Promise<SceneIssue[]>;

}>(recording: ReleaseRecordingRef, releases: readonly R[]): R | null;

type AssetEntry = {
    bytes?: number;
    created: {
        at: number;
        by: string;
    };
    deleted?: {
        at: number;
        by: string;
    };
    id: AssetId;
    import?: Record<string, unknown>;
    key: AssetKey;
    kind: AssetKindName;
    licence?: Licence;
    meta?: Record<string, unknown>;
    name?: string;
    path: AssetPath;
    sha256?: Sha256;
};

type AssetPath = string;

    buildAt: number;

    hash: Sha256;
};

    };

        "192"?: AssetId;
        "512"?: AssetId;
    };

        module: string;

type ProjectId = string;
type ProjectState = {
    assets: Readonly<Record<AssetKey, AssetEntry>>;
    build: Readonly<Record<AssetKindName, BuildStamp>>;
    code: Readonly<Record<string, CodeModuleInfo>>;
    history: Readonly<Record<AssetKey, readonly HashHistoryEntry[]>>;
    meta: {
        layout: number;
        name: string;
        projectId: ProjectId;
    };
};
type ProjectTreeNode = {
    children: ProjectTreeNode[];
    path: string;
    type: "folder";
} | {
    entry: AssetEntry;
    type: "asset";
} | {
    info: CodeModuleInfo;
    path: string;
    type: "code";
};
type ProjectView = {
    byId: ReadonlyMap<AssetId, AssetEntry>;
    byKey: ReadonlyMap<AssetKey, AssetEntry>;
    byPath: ReadonlyMap<AssetPath, AssetEntry>;
    deleted: readonly AssetEntry[];
    engine?: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
    issues: readonly SceneIssue[];
    repairs: ReadonlyMap<AssetKey, {
        id?: AssetId;
        path?: AssetPath;
    }>;
    state: ProjectState;
    tree(): ProjectTreeNode[];
};
type Sha256 = string;

    error: string;

    ok: true;
};
type RuntimeRequests = {
    flow: FlowRequest | null;
    saves: SaveRequest[];
};

    backend: "geometry" | "rapier";

type SceneRuntime = {
    add: (entities: RuntimeEntity[]) => ReconcileReport;
    busy: () => boolean;
    destroy: () => void;
    get: (id: string) => RuntimeEntity | undefined;
    load: (scene: ExpandedScene, play?: PlayOptions) => LoadReport;
    remove: (ids: string[]) => void;
    snapshot: () => unknown;
    stats: () => RuntimeStats;
    step: (dtMs: number, input: InputAction[] | TickInput, commands?: readonly GameCommand[]) => StepResult;
    takeRequests: () => RuntimeRequests;
    unload: () => void;
    update: (entities: RuntimeEntity[]) => ReconcileReport;
};

    readonly ctx: EngineContext;
    readonly game: GameRuntime | null;

};

    dev?: boolean;

    registry?: Registry;
    sampling?: "linear" | "nearest";
    scenes?: (() => SceneLibrary) | SceneLibrary;
    timing?: RunTiming;
    view: SceneView | null;
};

}

    bytes: number;

    ok: false;

## scene/checks.ts
declare const CHECKS_FORMAT_VERSION = 3;
declare const CHECKS_SUFFIX = ".checks.json";
type CheckCell = [
    col: number,
    row: number
];
type CheckHead = {
    id: string;
    kind: string;
    skip?: string;
};
type CheckPlace = CheckCell | Ref;
type ChecksFile = {
    $schema?: string;
    checks: SceneCheck[];
    v: typeof CHECKS_FORMAT_VERSION;
};
type ChecksIssue = {
    expected?: string;
    got?: string;
    hint?: string;
    line?: number;
    message: string;
    path: string;
};
type ConnectedCheck = CheckHead & {
    blockedBy?: string[];
    diagonals?: boolean;
    from: CheckPlace;
    kind: "connected";
    to: CheckPlace[];
};
declare const DEFAULT_BLOCKED_BY: readonly string[];
declare const DEFAULT_TOUCH_ROLLOUTS = 100;
declare const DEFAULT_TOUCH_SEED = 1;
declare const DEFAULT_TOUCH_TICKS = 600;
type GameCheck = CheckHead & {
    [field: string]: unknown;
};
declare const MAX_TOUCH_ROLLOUTS = 10000;
type ParseChecksResult = {
    file: ChecksFile;
    ok: true;
} | {
    issues: ChecksIssue[];
    ok: false;
};
type ReachesCheck = CheckHead & {
    at?: Pt;
    expect?: UntilClause;
    kind: "reaches";
    result: string;
    script: InputScript | InputScriptV2 | string;
};
type SceneCheck = ConnectedCheck | GameCheck | ReachesCheck | TouchesCheck | ValidCheck;
type TouchesCheck = CheckHead & {
    atLeast: number;
    entity: Ref;
    kind: "touches";
    rollouts?: number;
    seed?: number | string;
    ticks?: number;
};
type ValidCheck = CheckHead & {
    kind: "valid";
    max?: "error" | "warn";
    physical?: boolean;
};
declare function checksJsonSchema(registry: Registry): Record<string, unknown>;
declare function checksPathOf(sceneFile: string): string;
declare function checksSchemaFor(registry: Registry, players?: PlayersDef | null): z.ZodType<ChecksFile>;
declare function checksSchemaId(registry: Registry): string;
declare function parseChecks(text: string, registry: Registry): ParseChecksResult;

    readonly rest: {
        as: "atomic";
    };
};
declare function comparePointers(a: string | undefined, b: string | undefined, doc?: unknown): number;

    lines: Map<string, number>;
    text: string;

## scene/fragment.ts
declare const FRAGMENT_KIND = "scene-fragment";
type ParseFragmentResult = {
    fragment: SceneFragment;
    issues: SceneIssue[];
    migratedFrom?: number;
    ok: true;
} | {
    issues: SceneIssue[];
    ok: false;
};

    issues: SceneIssue[];
    ops: SceneOp[];
    select: Ref[];
};

    bulk?: Record<BulkKey, BulkEntry[]>;
    entities: EntityDef[];
    game?: string;
    instances?: InstanceDef[];
    kind: typeof FRAGMENT_KIND;
    v: number;
};

declare function nextFreeId(id: string, taken: ReadonlySet<string>): string;
declare function parseFragment(input: unknown, registry: Registry, opts?: ParseOptions): ParseFragmentResult;
declare function placeFragment(fragment: SceneFragment, def: SceneDefinition, registry: Registry, by: Pt, prefabs?: PrefabLibrary): PlacedFragment;
declare function takenIds(def: SceneDefinition): Set<string>;

    ok: false;
};
type Migration = (raw: unknown, env: MigrateEnv) => unknown;
type MigrationRefusal = {
    readonly refused: SceneIssue;
};
declare function isMigrationRefusal(value: unknown): value is MigrationRefusal;
declare function migrate(raw: unknown, migrations?: Readonly<Record<number, Migration>>, target?: number, env?: MigrateEnv): MigrateResult;

    ok: false;

    constructor(issues: SceneIssue[]);
}

    spawn?: boolean;

    prefab: Prefab;
} | {
    issues: SceneIssue[];
    ok: false;
};

## scene/recording-schema.ts
type ParseRecordingResult = {
    issues: RecordingIssue[];
    ok: false;
} | {
    migratedFrom?: 1;
    ok: true;
    recording: RunRecording;
};
declare const RECORDING_SCHEMA_ID = "urn:jams-engine:recording:v2";
declare const RECORDING_TRAIL_EVERY = 4;
declare const RECORDING_VERSION = 2;
type RecordingCommand = NonNullable<RunRecordingV1["commands"]>[number];
type RecordingIssue = {
    message: string;
    path: string;
};
type RecordingMarker = RunRecordingV1["markers"][number];
type RecordingPlatform = {
    arch: string;
    flavour: null | string;
    runtime: string;
};
type RecordingRegistry = Pick<Registry, "time"> & Partial<Pick<Registry, "commands" | "game">>;
type RecordingStream = Pick<RunRecordingV2, "actions" | "commands" | "inputs" | "system">;
type RunRecording = RunRecordingV2;
type RunRecordingV1 = z.infer<typeof runRecordingV1Schema>;
type RunRecordingV2 = {
    $schema?: string;
    actions: string[];
    commands: string[];
    configHash: string;
    end: string;
    engine: {
        physics: null | string;
        version: string;
    };
    fromSave?: {
        sha256: Sha256;
        slot: string;
    };
    game: null | string;
    inputs: SeatEvent[][];
    markers: RecordingMarker[];
    next?: {
        entry?: string;
        scene: AssetId;
    };
    outcome: RunRecordingV1["outcome"];
    platform: RecordingPlatform;
    players: {
        devices: DeviceId[];
        index: number;
    }[];
    project: null | {
        id: string;
        snapshot: null | string;
    };
    release?: Sha256;
    runHash: string;
    scene: string;
    sceneHash: string;
    seed: number | string;
    start: {
        at?: [
            x: number,
            y: number
        ];
        entry?: string;
        game?: unknown;
        persist?: JsonValue;
        timeOffsetMs?: number;
    };
    system: SystemEvent[];
    ticks: number;
    time: {
        kind: TimeKind;
        rate: number;
    };
    trail?: RunRecordingV1["trail"];
    v: 2;
};
type SeatEvent = [
    t: number,
    kind: "a",
    action: number,
    value: number
] | [
    t: number,
    kind: "b",
    action: number,
    down: 0 | 1
] | [
    t: number,
    kind: "c",
    command: number,
    args?: unknown
] | [
    t: number,
    kind: "p",
    x: number,
    y: number,
    buttons: number,
    over: 0 | 1
] | [
    t: number,
    kind: "v",
    action: number,
    x: number,
    y: number
];
type SystemEvent = [
    t: number,
    kind: "j",
    player: number,
    device: DeviceId
] | [
    t: number,
    kind: "l",
    player: number,
    device: DeviceId
] | [
    t: number,
    kind: "w",
    ms: number
];
declare function migrateRecordingV1(v1: RunRecordingV1, registry?: RecordingRegistry): RunRecordingV2;
declare function parseRecording(input: unknown, registry?: RecordingRegistry): ParseRecordingResult;
declare function recordingJsonSchema(): Record<string, unknown>;

type EngineIssueCode = (typeof ENGINE_ISSUE_CODE_LIST)[number];
type EntityBase = {
    group?: string;
    id: string;
    layer?: string;
    name?: string;
    off?: boolean;
    type: EntityTypeName;
    z?: number;
};
type EntityDef = EntityBase & {
    [field: string]: unknown;
};
type EntityInput = Omit<EntityBase, "id"> & {
    [field: string]: unknown;
    id?: string;
};
type EntityTypeName = string;
type ExpandedScene = {
    bounds: Bounds;
    entities: RuntimeEntity[];
    game?: string;
    name: string;
    settings: ResolvedSettings;
};
type FieldPath = string;
type GridSettings = {
    cell: [
        w: number,
        h: number
    ];
    index: "even" | "odd";
    kind: "hex";
    origin?: Pt;
    side: number;
    stagger: "x" | "y";
} | {
    cell: [
        w: number,
        h: number
    ];
    kind: "square";
    origin?: Pt;
};
type GroupDef = {
    at: Pt;
    flip?: true;
    name?: string;
    rot?: 1 | 2 | 3;
};
type InstanceDef = {
    at: Pt;
    flip?: true;
    group?: string;
    id: string;
    layer?: string;
    name?: string;
    off?: boolean;
    overrides?: Record<OverridePath, unknown>;
    params?: Record<string, unknown>;
    prefab: string;
    rot?: 1 | 2 | 3;
    z?: number;
};
type InstanceInput = Omit<InstanceDef, "id"> & {
    id?: string;
};
type IssueCode = EngineIssueCode | `${string}/${string}`;
type IssueCodeInfo = {
    fixable: boolean;
    hint: string;
    severity: Severity;
    summary: string;
};

    $schema?: string;
    game?: string;
    id: string;
    kind: typeof PREFAB_KIND;
    v: number;
};
type PrefabBody = {
    assets?: AssetId[];

    params?: Record<string, PrefabParam>;
};
type PrefabLibrary = ReadonlyMap<string, Prefab>;

    default?: unknown;

    x: number,
    y: number
];

    top: number;

type Ref = string;
type ResolvedSettings = {
    [key: string]: unknown;

    view: {
        projection: "orthogonal";
    };
};
type RuntimeEntity<F extends object = {
    [field: string]: unknown;
}> = {
    behaviors?: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>>>;
    id: string;
    layer?: string;
    transform?: {
        flip: boolean;
        turn: 0 | 1 | 2 | 3;
    };
    type: EntityTypeName;
    z?: number;
} & RuntimeFields<F>;
type RuntimeField<T> = T extends ShapeTuple ? ShapeObj : T;
type RuntimeFields<F> = {
    [K in keyof F]: RuntimeField<F[K]>;
};
declare const SCENE_FORMAT_VERSION = 4;

    "$schema",

    "settings",
    "assets",
    "groups",
    "instances"
];
type SceneChange = {
    by: Pt;
    kind: "moved";
    ref: Ref;
} | {
    field: "assets" | "bounds" | "game" | "name" | "v" | `groups.${string}` | `settings.${string}`;
    from: unknown;
    kind: "scene";
    to: unknown;
} | {
    field: FieldPath;
    from: unknown;
    kind: "modified";
    ref: Ref;
    to: unknown;
} | {
    from: BulkEntry;
    kind: "resized";
    ref: Ref;
    to: BulkEntry;
} | {
    from: string;
    kind: "renamed";
    to: string;
} | {
    kind: "added";
    ref: Ref;
    type: EntityTypeName;
} | {
    kind: "removed";
    ref: Ref;
    type: EntityTypeName;
};
type SceneDefinition = {
    $schema?: string;
    [bulkKey: BulkKey]: unknown;

    bounds: Bounds;
    entities?: EntityDef[];
    game?: string;
    groups?: Record<string, GroupDef>;
    instances?: InstanceDef[];
    name: string;
    settings?: SceneSettings;
    v: number;
};
type SceneHash = string;
type SceneIssue = {
    at?: Pt;
    code: IssueCode;
    detail?: Record<string, unknown>;
    entityIds: string[];
    expected?: string;
    fix?: OpBatch;
    got?: string;
    hint?: string;
    line?: number;
    message: string;
    path?: string;
    severity: Severity;
};
type SceneOp = {
    about?: Pt;
    flip?: "x" | "y";
    groups?: string[];
    op: "transform";
    refs?: string[];
    turn?: -1 | 1 | 2 | 3;
} | {
    add?: AssetId[];
    op: "useAssets";
    remove?: AssetId[];
} | {
    anchor?: SpotAnchor;
    at: Pt;
    op: "place";
    ref: Ref;
} | {
    at?: Pt;
    flip?: boolean;
    id: string;
    name?: null | string;
    op: "setGroup";
    remove?: boolean;
    rot?: 0 | 1 | 2 | 3;
} | {
    at?: Pt;
    id: string;
    instance?: string;
    op: "createPrefab";
    refs: Ref[];
} | {
    base: null | string;
    op: "setPrefabBase";
} | {
    by: Pt;
    op: "move";
    refs: Ref[];
} | {
    by?: number;
    op: "setZ";
    refs: Ref[];
    z?: number;
} | {
    cells?: CellEdit[];
    field: string;
    op: "paintCells";
    rects?: CellRect[];
    ref: Ref;
} | {
    entity: EntityInput;
    index?: number;
    op: "add";
} | {
    from: string;
    op: "rename";
    to: string;
} | {
    id: string;
    op: "definePrefab";
    prefab: PrefabBody | null;
} | {
    id: string;
    op: "demote";
} | {
    id: string;
    op: "promote";
    ref: Ref;
} | {
    index: number;
    op: "reorder";
    refs: Ref[];
} | {
    index?: number;
    instance: InstanceInput;
    op: "instantiate";
} | {
    index?: number;
    key: BulkKey;
    op: "addBulk";
    points: Pt[];
} | {
    index?: number;
    key: BulkKey;
    op: "addBulk";
    shapes: ShapeInput[];
} | {
    name: string;
    op: "exposeParam";
    param: PrefabParam | null;
} | {
    name: string;
    op: "setParam";
    ref: string;
    value: unknown;
} | {
    op: "applyToPrefab";
    paths?: OverridePath[];
    ref: Ref;
    to?: string;
} | {
    op: "override";
    ref?: Ref;
    set: Record<OverridePath, unknown>;
} | {
    op: "remove";
    refs: Ref[];
} | {
    op: "revertOverride";
    paths?: OverridePath[];
    ref?: Ref;
} | {
    op: "setScene";
    set: {
        bounds?: Bounds;
        name?: string;
    };
} | {
    op: "setSettings";
    set?: Record<FieldPath, unknown>;
    unset?: FieldPath[];
} | {
    op: "unpack";
    ref: Ref;
} | {
    op: "update";
    ref: Ref;
    set?: Record<FieldPath, unknown>;
    unset?: FieldPath[];
};
type SceneSettings = {
    [key: string]: unknown;
    gravity?: Pt;
    grid?: GridSettings;
    physics?: "none" | "rapier";
    view?: {
        projection: "orthogonal";
    };
};
type Severity = "error" | "info" | "warn";
type ShapeInput = RectInput | ShapeObj | ShapeTuple;
type ShapeObj = {
    cx: number;
    cy: number;
    halfLen: number;
    r: number;
    rotDeg: number;
    type: "capsule";
} | {
    cx: number;
    cy: number;
    hh: number;
    hw: number;
    rotDeg: number;
    type: "box";
} | {
    cx: number;
    cy: number;
    r: number;
    type: "circle";
} | {
    pts: Pt[];
    type: "chain";
} | {
    pts: Pt[];
    type: "poly";
};
type ShapeTuple = [
    "box",
    cx: number,
    cy: number,
    hw: number,
    hh: number
] | [
    "box",
    cx: number,
    cy: number,
    hw: number,
    hh: number,
    rotDeg: number
] | [
    "capsule",
    cx: number,
    cy: number,
    halfLen: number,
    r: number
] | [
    "capsule",
    cx: number,
    cy: number,
    halfLen: number,
    r: number,
    rotDeg: number
] | [
    "chain",
    ...coords: number[]
] | [
    "circle",
    cx: number,
    cy: number,
    r: number
] | [
    "poly",
    ...coords: number[]
];
type ShapeType = ShapeObj["type"];
type SpotAnchor = "bottom" | "center" | "left" | "right" | "top";

## standalone/host-channel.ts
type ConnectHostOptions = {
    hello: {
        engine: string;
        game: string;
        release: Sha256;
    };
    requestTimeoutMs?: number;
    timeoutMs?: number;
    welcomeTimeoutMs?: number;
    window?: Window;
};
type HostChannel = {
    close(): void;
    offerDownload(name: string, contentType: string, bytes: ArrayBuffer): void;
    on<E extends keyof HostEvents>(event: E, fn: (data: HostEvents[E]) => void): () => void;
    readonly dropped: number;
    readonly welcome: HostWelcome;
    request<M extends HostRequestMethod>(method: M, params: HostRequestParams<M>): Promise<HostRequestResult<M>>;
    state(state: "error" | "loading" | "ready", issue?: SceneIssue): void;
};

}

## standalone/host-protocol.ts
type FrameMessage = z.infer<typeof frameMessageSchema>;
declare const HOST_DOWNLOAD_MAX_BYTES: number;
declare const HOST_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS = 2000;
declare const HOST_MESSAGE_MAX_BYTES = 65536;
declare const HOST_PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1;
declare const HOST_REQUESTS: {
    readonly "prefs.save": {
        readonly params: z.ZodObject<{
            store: z.ZodEnum<{
                prefs: "prefs";
                remap: "remap";
            }>;
            value: z.ZodUnknown;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        readonly result: z.ZodNull;
    };
    readonly "saves.list": {
        readonly params: z.ZodObject<{
            gameId: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        readonly result: z.ZodArray<z.ZodObject<{
            bytes: z.ZodNumber;
            meta: z.ZodObject<{
                playTicks: z.ZodNumber;
                savedAt: z.ZodNumber;
                scene: z.ZodString;
                title: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
            }, z.core.$strip>;
            slot: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strip>>;
    };
    readonly "saves.read": {
        readonly params: z.ZodObject<{
            gameId: z.ZodString;
            slot: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        readonly result: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
    };
    readonly "saves.remove": {
        readonly params: z.ZodObject<{
            gameId: z.ZodString;
            slot: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        readonly result: z.ZodNull;
    };
    readonly "saves.write": {
        readonly params: z.ZodObject<{
            gameId: z.ZodString;
            slot: z.ZodString;
            text: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        readonly result: z.ZodUnion<readonly [
            z.ZodObject<{
                bytes: z.ZodNumber;
                ok: z.ZodLiteral<true>;
            }, z.core.$strip>,
            z.ZodObject<{
                issue: z.ZodType<SceneIssue, unknown, z.core.$ZodTypeInternals<SceneIssue, unknown>>;
                ok: z.ZodLiteral<false>;
            }, z.core.$strip>
        ]>;
    };
    readonly "session.token": {
        readonly params: z.ZodObject<{
            sessionId: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        readonly result: z.ZodObject<{
            token: z.ZodString;
            url: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strip>;
    };
    readonly hello: {
        readonly params: z.ZodObject<{
            engine: z.ZodString;
            game: z.ZodString;
            release: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        readonly result: z.ZodObject<{
            online: z.ZodBoolean;
            player: z.ZodObject<{
                name: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
                signedIn: z.ZodBoolean;
            }, z.core.$strip>;
            prefs: z.ZodObject<{
                prefs: z.ZodUnknown;
                remap: z.ZodUnknown;
            }, z.core.$strip>;
            saves: z.ZodObject<{
                describe: z.ZodString;
                limits: z.ZodObject<{
                    maxBytes: z.ZodNumber;
                    maxSlots: z.ZodNumber;
                }, z.core.$strict>;
                mode: z.ZodEnum<{
                    none: "none";
                    own: "own";
                    site: "site";
                }>;
            }, z.core.$strip>;
        }, z.core.$strip>;
    };
};
declare const HOST_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 30000;
declare const HOST_WELCOME_TIMEOUT_MS = 30000;
type HostEvents = {
    "saves.changed": HostSaves;
    pause: undefined;
    resume: undefined;
};
type HostMessage = z.infer<typeof hostMessageSchema>;
type HostRequestMethod = keyof typeof HOST_REQUESTS;
type HostRequestParams<M extends HostRequestMethod> = z.infer<(typeof HOST_REQUESTS)[M]["params"]>;
type HostRequestResult<M extends HostRequestMethod> = z.infer<(typeof HOST_REQUESTS)[M]["result"]>;
type HostSaves = z.infer<typeof hostSavesSchema>;
type HostWelcome = z.infer<typeof welcomeSchema>;
declare const frameMessageSchema: z.ZodDiscriminatedUnion<[
    z.ZodObject<{
        id: z.ZodNumber;
        params: z.ZodObject<{
            engine: z.ZodString;
            game: z.ZodString;
            release: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        type: z.ZodLiteral<"hello">;
    }, z.core.$strict>,
    z.ZodObject<{
        id: z.ZodNumber;
        params: z.ZodObject<{
            gameId: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        type: z.ZodLiteral<"saves.list">;
    }, z.core.$strict>,
    z.ZodObject<{
        id: z.ZodNumber;
        params: z.ZodObject<{
            gameId: z.ZodString;
            slot: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        type: z.ZodLiteral<"saves.read">;
    }, z.core.$strict>,
    z.ZodObject<{
        id: z.ZodNumber;
        params: z.ZodObject<{
            gameId: z.ZodString;
            slot: z.ZodString;
            text: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        type: z.ZodLiteral<"saves.write">;
    }, z.core.$strict>,
    z.ZodObject<{
        id: z.ZodNumber;
        params: z.ZodObject<{
            gameId: z.ZodString;
            slot: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        type: z.ZodLiteral<"saves.remove">;
    }, z.core.$strict>,
    z.ZodObject<{
        id: z.ZodNumber;
        params: z.ZodObject<{
            store: z.ZodEnum<{
                prefs: "prefs";
                remap: "remap";
            }>;
            value: z.ZodUnknown;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        type: z.ZodLiteral<"prefs.save">;
    }, z.core.$strict>,
    z.ZodObject<{

            sessionId: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strict>;
        type: z.ZodLiteral<"session.token">;
    }, z.core.$strict>,
    z.ZodObject<{
        issue: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodType<SceneIssue, unknown, z.core.$ZodTypeInternals<SceneIssue, unknown>>>;
        jamsState: z.ZodEnum<{
            error: "error";
            loading: "loading";
            ready: "ready";
        }>;
        type: z.ZodLiteral<"state">;
    }, z.core.$strict>,
    z.ZodObject<{
        bytes: z.ZodCustom<ArrayBuffer, ArrayBuffer>;
        contentType: z.ZodString;
        name: z.ZodString;
        type: z.ZodLiteral<"download">;
    }, z.core.$strict>
], "type">;
declare const frameReadySchema: z.ZodObject<{
    protocol: z.ZodLiteral<1>;

    config(): ExportBuildConfig;
    configResolved(config: {
        root: string;
    }): void;
    enforce?: "pre";
    generateBundle: {
        handler(this: {
            emitFile(f: {
                fileName: string;
                source: Uint8Array | string;
                type: "asset";
            }): void;
        }, opts: unknown, bundle: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;
        order: "post";
    };
    load(id: string): null | string;
    name: string;
    resolveId(id: string): null | string;
};

    from: DiskEvent;
    to: DiskEvent;
    type: "move";
